Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on dp structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 286. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 961 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to dp structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 286/961 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in dp theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 61 and the invariant I(A,B) = 228 mod 1.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the dp space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 61 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 228. QED.
